and
(ii)
in the third step,
5.31
in the fourth, and
5.33
in the last step. On the one hand,

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}}-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{1}}+\frac{d}{\tilde{s}_{2}}}\right)q=(1-C)\frac{q}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}}<0,\quad\text{since}\quad C>1.

Therefore, the geometric sum

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{n=0}^{\infty}2^{n\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}}-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{1}}+\frac{d}{\tilde{s}_{2}}}\right)q}\|f\|_{\widehat{B}^{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T})}^{q}\sim_{\,d,p,s_{1},s_{2},\alpha_{2}}\|f\|_{\widehat{B}^{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T})}^{q}

(42)

converges. On the other hand,

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{n=0}^{\infty}2^{n\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}}-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{1}}+\frac{d}{\tilde{s}_{2}}}\right)q}\left(\sum\limits_{k=0}^{n}\left(2^{\frac{k}{\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{1}}+\frac{d}{\tilde{s}_{2}}}}\sigma_{2^{k}}(f)_{\widehat{B}^{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T})}\right)^{\tilde{q}^{*}}\right)^{\frac{q}{\tilde{q}^{*}}}=\sum\limits_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{(-n)\theta q}b_{-n}^{q}

(43)

with
\displaystyle{b_{m}:=\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{Z}^{-}_{0}}(\{m\})\left(\sum\limits_{k=0}^{-m}\left(2^{\frac{k}{\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{1}}+\frac{d}{\tilde{s}_{2}}}}\sigma_{2^{k}}(f)_{\widehat{B}^{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T})}\right)^{\tilde{q}^{*}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}^{*}}}}
,
m\in\mathbb{Z}
,
\theta:=\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{1}}+\frac{d}{\tilde{s}_{2}}}-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}}\right)>0
, and
\mathbb{Z}^{-}_{0}:=\{k\in\mathbb{Z}\mid k\leq 0\}
. Since,
b_{m}=\left(\sum\limits_{k=m}^{\infty}a_{k}^{\tilde{q}^{*}}\right)^{\frac{1}{\tilde{q}^{*}}}
,
m\in\mathbb{Z}
, for
a_{k}:=\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{Z}^{-}_{0}}(\{k\})2^{-\frac{k}{\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{1}}+\frac{d}{\tilde{s}_{2}}}}\sigma_{2^{-k}}(f)_{\widehat{B}^{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T})}
using the discrete Hardy inequality as in
[
DL93
, Lem. 3.4 of Ch. 2]
yields

\displaystyle\sum\limits_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{(-n)\theta q}b_{-n}^{q}\lesssim_{\,\begin{subarray}{c}d,p,s_{1},\\
s_{2},\alpha_{2}\end{subarray}}\sum\limits_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}2^{n\theta q}a_{n}^{q}=\sum\limits_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{Z}^{-}_{0}}(\{n\})\,2^{n\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{1}}+\frac{d}{\tilde{s}_{2}}}-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}}\right)q}2^{n\left(-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\tilde{s}_{1}}+\frac{d}{\tilde{s}_{2}}}\right)q}\sigma_{2^{-n}}(f)_{\widehat{B}^{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T})}^{q}

(44)

\displaystyle=\sum\limits_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\mathds{1}_{\mathbb{Z}^{-}_{0}}(\{n\})\,2^{-n\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}}\right)q}\sigma_{2^{-n}}(f)_{\widehat{B}^{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T})}^{q}=\sum\limits_{n=0}^{\infty}2^{n\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}}\right)q}\sigma_{2^{n}}(f)_{\widehat{B}^{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T})}^{q}\leq|f|_{\mathbb{A}_{\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}},q}\left(\widehat{B}^{\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T})\right)}^{q}.

Now inserting (
42
) and (
43
) together with (
44
) into (
5.3
), yields the first asserted embedding. Thus, for the above choice of
(s_{1}^{\prime},s_{2}^{\prime})
, this, in particular, means

\displaystyle\mathbb{A}_{\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}},q}\Big(\widehat{B}^{s_{1}^{\prime}-s_{1},s_{2}^{\prime}-s_{2}}_{p,p}(\Omega_{T}),\mathbb{V}^{r_{1},r_{2}}_{\bullet},\textup{PATCH}\_\textup{REFINE}(\cdot,\cdot,d,s_{1},s_{2})\Big)\hookrightarrow\widehat{B}^{s_{1}^{\prime},s_{2}^{\prime}}_{q,q}(\Omega_{T}).

Now, the assertion follows from
5.34
, since
s_{i}^{\prime}-s_{i}<0
,
i=1,2
.
∎

Appendix A
Algebraic calculation in
5.14

We observe that due to the collinearity of
(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})
and
(s_{1},s_{2})
, there is a constant
C>0
such that
(s_{1},s_{2})=C(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2})
. This implies

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}+s_{1}}+\frac{d}{\alpha_{2}+s_{2}}}=\frac{1}{\frac{1}{(1+C)\alpha_{1}}+\frac{d}{(1+C)\alpha_{2}}}=(1+C)\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}}+\frac{d}{\alpha_{2}}}=(1+C)\frac{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2}}{\alpha_{2}+d\alpha_{1}}=\frac{\alpha_{1}(\alpha_{2}+s_{2})}{\alpha_{2}+d\alpha_{1}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{2}}{\alpha_{1}d}\right)\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}+s_{1}}+\frac{d}{\alpha_{2}+s_{2}}}=\frac{\alpha_{1}(\alpha_{2}+s_{2})}{\alpha_{1}d}=\frac{\alpha_{2}+s_{2}}{d},

which yields

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha_{2}}{d}-\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{2}}{\alpha_{1}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}+s_{1}}+\frac{d}{\alpha_{2}+s_{2}}}-\frac{1}{q}+\frac{1}{p}\right)=-\frac{s_{2}}{d}-\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{2}}{\alpha_{1}d}\right)\left(-\frac{1}{q}+\frac{1}{p}\right).

Moreover,

\displaystyle\frac{s_{2}}{d}=\frac{s_{1}d+s_{2}}{s_{1}d}\frac{s_{1}s_{2}}{s_{2}+s_{1}d}=\left(1+\frac{s_{2}}{s_{1}d}\right)\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}},

which together with
\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{2}}{\alpha_{1}d}\right)=\left(1+\frac{s_{2}}{s_{1}d}\right)
, due to the collinearity, gives

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha_{2}}{d}-\left(1+\frac{\alpha_{2}}{\alpha_{1}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{\alpha_{1}+s_{1}}+\frac{d}{\alpha_{2}+s_{2}}}-\frac{1}{q}+\frac{1}{p}\right)=-\left(1+\frac{s_{2}}{s_{1}d}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\frac{1}{s_{1}}+\frac{d}{s_{2}}}-\frac{1}{q}+\frac{1}{p}\right).
